Bennington: 72 hours of minute simulation

100 residents. 72 seconds of real time in this test. No Language Model calls.

Counts and hourly checks cover the full run. The readable appendix contains up to 180 representative events per sampled Sim; the actual simulation keeps every event.

The audit checks bounded needs and age rules hourly, as well as repeated interruption loops across the complete town. This is a representative review, not proof that every possible future behavior is plausible.
